 P9 J3 J4 100 6 100

 V2 J1 J4 6 GPV G1
 V3 J3 J4 6 FCV 100
[PATTERNS]

[CURVES]

 G1 0 0
 G1 1000 10
[RULES]
RULE A
IF TANK T1 LEVEL > 12
THEN PUMP U1 STATUS IS CLOSED
ELSE PUMP U1 STATUS IS OPEN
PRIORITY 1
RULE B
IF SYSTEM CLOCKTIME >= 1 AM
AND SYSTEM CLOCKTIME < 3:00 AM
THEN VALVE V1 SETTING = 30
AND VALVE V3 SETTING = 60
RULE C
IF JUNCTION J1 HEAD BELOW 90
OR RESERVOIR R1 GRADE ABOVE 200
OR NODE J2 PRESSURE <> 0
THEN PIPE P2 STATUS IS CLOSED
ELSE PIPE P2 STATUS IS OPEN
RULE D
IF LINK P1 FLOW <= 1e6
AND PIPE P1 STATUS = OPEN
AND PUMP U1 STATUS NOT CLOSED
AND VALVE V1 STATUS IS ACTIVE
AND VALVE V1 SETTING >= 0
AND JUNCTION J3 DEMAND >= 0
THEN PUMP U1 SETTING = 1.1
RULE E
IF SYSTEM TIME = 2
OR SYSTEM TIME <> 3
THEN VALVE V2 STATUS IS OPEN
AND PUMP U1 STATUS IS OPEN
RULE F
IF SYSTEM DEMAND > 0
AND TANK T1 FILLTIME > 0
AND TANK T1 DRAINTIME < 100
THEN PIPE P3 SETTING = 0
PRIORITY 2
RULE G
IF SYSTEM TIME >= 1:00
AND SYSTEM TIME <= 5
AND SYSTEM TIME < 6
THEN PIPE P3 STATUS IS OPEN
PRIORITY 3
RULE H
IF SYSTEM CLOCKTIME = 2 AM
OR SYSTEM CLOCKTIME <> 4 AM
OR SYSTEM CLOCKTIME > 12 PM
OR SYSTEM CLOCKTIME <= 1 PM
THEN VALVE V1 STATUS IS CLOSED
AND VALVE V3 STATUS IS OPEN
RULE I
IF TANK T1 LEVEL >= 0
THEN VALVE V3 SETTING = 50
ELSE VALVE V3 SETTING = 55
PRIORITY 0.5
RULE J
IF TANK T1 LEVEL >= 0
THEN PIPE P2 STATUS IS CLOSED
DISABLED
RULE K
IF TANK T1 HEAD >= 0
AND TANK T1 GRADE < 1e6
THEN PIPE P2 SETTING = 1
[TIMES]

 Hydraulic Timestep 0:30

[OPTIONS]
